+class TestGWizardDiffHighlight(unittest.TestCase):
+    """Test cases for the diff-line row highlight used by the merge dialog."""
+
+    def test_diff_line_rule_defines_background(self) -> None:
+        """The dialog stylesheet must style .diff-line with a background."""
+        from gramps.gui.gwizard.gwizardmergedialog import (
+            DIFF_CSS_DATA,
+            DIFF_STYLE_CLASS,
+        )
+
+        css_text = DIFF_CSS_DATA.decode("utf-8")
+        self.assertIn(".%s" % DIFF_STYLE_CLASS, css_text)
+        rule_start = css_text.index(".%s" % DIFF_STYLE_CLASS)
+        rule = css_text[rule_start:]
+        self.assertIn("background-color", rule[: rule.find("}")])
+
+    def test_diff_css_parses(self) -> None:
+        """The dialog stylesheet data must parse without GLib error."""
+        from gi.repository import Gtk
+
+        from gramps.gui.gwizard.gwizardmergedialog import DIFF_CSS_DATA
+
+        provider = Gtk.CssProvider()
+        provider.load_from_data(DIFF_CSS_DATA)
+
+    def test_ensure_diff_styles_installed_without_screen(self) -> None:
+        """Installing the stylesheet must be safe in a headless run."""
+        from gi.repository import Gdk
+
+        from gramps.gui.gwizard import gwizardmergedialog
+
+        saved = gwizardmergedialog._DIFF_CSS_INSTALLED
+        try:
+            gwizardmergedialog._DIFF_CSS_INSTALLED = False
+            result = gwizardmergedialog.ensure_diff_styles_installed()
+        finally:
+            gwizardmergedialog._DIFF_CSS_INSTALLED = saved
+
+        self.assertIsInstance(result, bool)
+        if Gdk.Screen.get_default() is None:
+            self.assertFalse(result)
+
+    @unittest.skipUnless(
+        _HAS_GTK_DISPLAY,
+        "needs a real Gtk display; building a widget without one crashes",
+    )
+    def test_diff_highlight_class_applied_to_differing_cells(self) -> None:
+        """Only the cells of a differing row may carry the diff-line class."""
+        from gramps.gui.gwizard.gwizardmergedialog import (
+            DIFF_STYLE_CLASS,
+            create_diff_cell,
+            field_values_differ,
+        )
+
+        differs = field_values_differ("Hansdotter", "Hansdotter Smith")
+        marked = create_diff_cell("<i>Surname: </i>Hansdotter", differs, 0.0)
+        self.assertTrue(marked.get_style_context().has_class(DIFF_STYLE_CLASS))
+        self.assertEqual(marked.get_text(), "Surname: Hansdotter")
+        self.assertEqual(marked.get_xalign(), 0.0)
+
+        matches = field_values_differ("Anna", "Anna")
+        plain = create_diff_cell("<i>Given Name: </i>Anna", matches, 0.0)
+        self.assertFalse(plain.get_style_context().has_class(DIFF_STYLE_CLASS))
+        self.assertEqual(plain.get_xalign(), 0.0)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+

+# ------------------------------------------------------------
+#
+# GWizardMergeTool
+#
+# ------------------------------------------------------------
+class GWizardMergeTool(tool.Tool):
+    """
+    Launch the GWizard Data Merge flow.
+
+    Thin wrapper around the shared launcher: ask for a source file,
+    load it, and open the side-by-side compare window. Identical to
+    the Family Trees menu entry.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        dbstate: Any,
+        user: Any,
+        options_class: Any,
+        name: str,
+        callback: Any = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        """Initialize the tool and run the shared merge flow."""
+        uistate = user.uistate
+        tool.Tool.__init__(self, dbstate, options_class, name)
+        # When installed as a standalone addon, sibling modules live in the
+        # addon directory, which Gramps removes from sys.path after import.
+        # Re-add it so "from gwizardlauncher import ..." keeps working here
+        # and inside the sibling modules themselves.
+        import os as _os
+        import sys as _sys
+
+        _addon_dir = _os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__))
+        if _addon_dir not in _sys.path:
+            _sys.path.insert(0, _addon_dir)
+        try:
+            from gwizardlauncher import run_gwizard_merge_flow
+        except ImportError:
+            # Running from the source tree: fall back to the core location.
+            from gramps.gui.gwizard.gwizardlauncher import (
+                run_gwizard_merge_flow,
+            )
+
+        run_gwizard_merge_flow(uistate, dbstate, parent=uistate.window)

+# GWizard GEDCOM Compare/Merge Functionality Explained
+
+This document describes the GEDCOM data hierarchy, how the GWizard import/merge framework compares and merges these elements, and what gaps must be addressed to ensure robust, production-grade operation in Gramps.
+
+---
+
+## 1. The GEDCOM Hierarchy
+
+GEDCOM (Genealogical Data Communication) is structured hierarchically. Lines begin with an integer level number (0, 1, 2, ...), followed by a cross-reference identifier (e.g., `@I1@`, `@F1@`) or a tag (e.g., `INDI`, `FAM`, `BIRT`), and finally any value or pointer reference.
+
+### 0-Level Records (Primary Objects in Gramps)
+- **Individual (`0 @I1@ INDI`)**: The core person record.
+  - Sub-records (Level 1+):
+    - `NAME`: Name structure (`GIVN` Given Name, `SURN` Surname, `_MARN` Married Name, etc.). Can have multiple `NAME` records for alternate names.
+    - `SEX`: Gender (`M`, `F`, `U`).
+    - `BIRT`, `DEAT`, `CHR`, `BAPM`: Individual-level events containing `DATE` and `PLAC` (Place).
+    - `OCCU`, `RESI`, `EDUC`: Personal attributes / facts.
+    - `FAMS`: Pointer to a Family record where the person is a spouse/parent.
+    - `FAMC`: Pointer to a Family record where the person is a child.
+    - `_FSFTID`: FamilySearch ID attribute.
+    - `NOTE @N1@`: Pointer to a Note record.
+    - `OBJE @M1@`: Pointer to a Media Object record.
+    - `SOUR @S1@`: Pointer to a Source record (linked via a Citation).
+- **Family (`0 @F1@ FAM`)**: Represents marriages, cohabitation, and family unions.
+  - Sub-records (Level 1+):
+    - `HUSB @I1@`: Pointer to the husband.
+    - `WIFE @I2@`: Pointer to the wife.
+    - `CHIL @I3@`: Pointer to a child.
+    - `MARR`, `DIV`: Family-level events containing `DATE` and `PLAC`.
+- **Source (`0 @S1@ SOUR`)** & **Citation**: Source citations.
+- **Media Object (`0 @M1@ OBJE`)**: References to external media files.
+- **Note (`0 @N1@ NOTE`)**: Text block records.
+- **Repository (`0 @R1@ REPO`)**: Source repository.
+
+---
+
+## 2. How GWizard Compare/Merge Works
+
+### Comparative Logic (`_compare` in `gedcom.py`)
+GWizard uses a two-stage parsing approach. First, it imports the entire external GEDCOM file into an isolated in-memory dictionary database (`source_db`).
+When a user selects a source person and a potential target person, `GedGWizard._compare` produces side-by-side rows representing differences:
+1. **Given Name**: Compares `first_name` on primary name.
+2. **Surname**: Compares surnames on primary name.
+3. **Gender**: Compares the gender code.
+4. **Vitals (Birth/Death Events)**: Compares date strings and place titles of birth and death events.
+5. **FamilySearch ID**: Compares the `_FSFTID` attribute value.
+6. **Relations**: Iterates through relatives (Father, Mother, Spouse, Children) and lists their details.
+7. **Events & Other Records**: Groups non-birth/death events by type and compares them.
+
+### Merging Logic (`_apply` in `gedcom.py`)
+When resolutions are applied:
+* **Add as New Person (`target_person_handle is None`)**:
+  - The source person is deep-copied (`copy.deepcopy(s_person)`).
+  - Its birth/death events and associated places are duplicated into the target database.
+  - The person is committed to the target database.
+* **Merge into Existing Person**:
+  - Direct field resolutions (`given_name`, `surname`, `gender`) are copied from the source person into the target person.
+  - Vitals events (`birth_event`, `death_event`) are copied using the `copy_event` helper.
+  - FamilySearch IDs are linked as a custom attribute (`_FSFTID`).
+  - Other events are duplicated and added as custom event references on the target person.
+  - Relatives (`father`, `mother`, `spouse`, `child`) are mapped using `best_target_person` (which scans the target database for similar records).
+  - The active database's Family records (`Family`) are updated or created to link these related handles.
+
+---
+
+## 3. Critical Gaps & Missing Functionality
+
+The following gaps must be resolved to prevent database corruption, broken links, or loss of information:
+
+### A. Dangling References / Shared Primary Objects (Highest Priority)
+- **Problem**: When a person or event is deep-copied (via `copy.deepcopy`), its note references (`note_ref_list`), media references (`media_ref_list`), and citations (`citation_ref_list`) are copied verbatim. However, the corresponding `Note`, `Media`, `Source`, and `Citation` records exist *only* in `source_db`. This leaves the target database with broken/dangling pointers (non-existent handles) which will trigger `HandleError` crashes in the GUI.
+- **Needed**: A recursive reference resolver in `_apply` that detects when an entity contains references to `Note`, `Media`, `Citation`, or `Source` records. It must copy those records from `source_db` to the target database and update the handles on the merged person or event accordingly.
+
+### B. Family-Level Events
+- **Problem**: GWizard handles the structural connections of a family (Husband, Wife, Children) but completely ignores family-level events like Marriage (`MARR`), Divorce (`DIV`), and Engagement (`ENGA`).
+- **Needed**: Side-by-side comparison and resolutions for family-level events, transferring these event records to the matched or newly created target `Family` record.
+
+### C. Alternate Names
+- **Problem**: In Gramps, individuals can have multiple names. Currently, `GWizard` only compares and merges the primary name. Any alternate names on the source record are lost during a merge (though they are preserved during a complete "add new" operation).
+- **Needed**: Logic to compare alternate names and merge new alternate name records into the target person's name list.
+
+### D. Personal Attributes (Facts)
+- **Problem**: Personal attributes (Occupation, Religion, Residence) are kept in the `attribute_list` of `Person`. These are not shown in `_compare` or handled during a merge.
+- **Needed**: Grouping, comparing, and merging of person attributes.
+
+### E. Place Merging and Deduplication
+- **Problem**: The `get_or_create_place` helper only checks for matches by exact title. It does not perform a deeper match of latitude, longitude, and place structure, leading to duplicate places.
+- **Needed**: Enhanced place matcher or integration with Gramps' standard place matching tools.

+# GWizard GEDCOM Compare/Merge Unit Test Plan
+
+This document outlines the testing strategy, test flows, and execution methods to comprehensively test the GWizard GEDCOM compare/merge framework.
+
+---
+
+## 1. Test Setup Strategy: Partial Database Merging
+
+**Do the unit tests start with a partial Gramps database and merge in all possible GEDCOM element data additions?**
+
+**Yes.** In order to accurately simulate a real user scenario, the test suite is designed to start with an active, partially populated, in-memory Gramps database (the target database) containing existing person records, events, and relationship structures. The tests then load an external GEDCOM file containing overlapping, conflicting, and new genealogical records, and run the GWizard matching, side-by-side comparison, and selective merge resolutions.
+
+### Initialization & Fixtures
+1. **Target Gramps DB**: Built in-memory using `make_database("sqlite")`. It is seeded with:
+   - A target individual (e.g., "John Doe") with specific vitals (birth date and place).
+   - Existing relative records (e.g., "Jane Doe" as a spouse, or parents/children) to test structural matching.
+2. **Source GEDCOM File**: A dynamically generated, minimal, valid GEDCOM text string containing diverse nodes and hierarchies (e.g., `INDI`, `FAM`, `BIRT`, `DEAT`, and spouse/child pointer blocks). It is written to a temporary `.ged` file using `tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile` during test execution.
+
+---
+
+## 2. Comprehensive Test Cases Outline
+
+The unit tests exercise the compare/merge logic across all critical components:
+
+### A. Match Engine (`CandidateMatcher`)
+- **Exact Matches**: Asserts that identical name and gender records yield high confidence scores (> 1.0).
+- **Inexact Matches**: Asserts that phonetic surname matching (using Soundex) and birth year differences calculate proper intermediate weights.
+- **Hard Mismatches**: Asserts that gender discrepancies or highly divergent details result in a negative score (-1.0), filtering them out as candidates.
+
+### B. End-to-End Import & Merge Flow (`GedGWizard`)
+The tests execute the step-by-step workflow (`connect` -> `load` -> `match` -> `compare` -> `apply`):
+1. **Connect**: Verifies file verification and error handling for missing/corrupt GEDCOM paths.
+2. **Load**: Verifies that `import_as_dict` successfully reads the GEDCOM into a temporary source DB and iterates all handles correctly.
+3. **Match**: Asserts that potential candidates in the target DB are surfaced with matching scores.
+4. **Compare**: Verifies that `_compare` yields the correct `GWizardCompareRow` records with precise statuses (`match`, `differ`, `source_only`, `target_only`) and data payloads.
+5. **Apply (Selective Merge)**:
+   - **Fields Overwrite**: Resolves fields with `"source"`, asserting that target fields are updated.
+   - **Fields Preserved**: Resolves fields with `"target"`, asserting that target fields remain untouched.
+   - **Adding entirely new people**: Resolves with a `None` target handle, asserting that a brand-new, deep-copied person is created in the target database.
+
+### C. Complex Relational Merging
+- **Spouse / Family Merging**: Verifies that merging a spouse relationship (`spouse:<source_handle>`) successfully resolves the relative's counterpart in the target DB, instantiates a `Family` object if one doesn't exist, and links HUSB/WIFE roles accordingly.
+- **Children / Parent Linking**: Asserts that merging child/parent relationships creates the proper family associations and updates parent/family reference lists.
+
+### D. UI Layout & Representation Logic (`GWizardMergeDialog`)
+- **Pango Text Highlighting**: Unit tests the headless static helper `_format_diff_line` of `GWizardMergeDialog`.
+  - Asserts that matching strings return plain strings.
+  - Asserts that word-level discrepancies are wrapped in Pango markup tags (`<i><b>...</b></i>`) for rendering in the GTK tree view.
+  - Verifies trailing punctuation handling and labels rendering.
+
+---
+
+## 3. Execution Instructions
+
+Unit tests are written using the standard `unittest` framework (no `pytest`). Because some GUI-related classes are imported, you must run the tests headlessly by disabling GDK displays:
+
+```bash
+# Headless environment setup & execution command
+export GDK_BACKEND=-
+export GRAMPS_RESOURCES=build/share
+export LANGUAGE=
+export LANG=en_US.utf-8
+python3 -m unittest gramps.plugins.tool.test.gwizard_test gramps.plugins.tool.test.gwizard_merge_fields_test gramps.plugins.tool.test.gwizard_styling_test gramps.plugins.tool.test.gwizardmerge_test
+```

+# GWizard GEDCOM Compare/Merge To-Do List
+
+This document lists the tasks and enhancements required to bring the GWizard GEDCOM import and merge functionality to a robust, production-grade, and error-free level.
+
+---
+
+## [HIGH PRIORITY] 1. Shared Primary Object Copying (Dangling Reference Fixes)
+- [x] **Recursive Reference Resolution**: When adding a new person or copying an event, detect and copy referenced secondary/shared objects to avoid broken handle pointers:
+  - [x] **Notes**: Import and add `Note` records referenced in `note_ref_list`.
+  - [x] **Media**: Import and add `Media` records referenced in `media_ref_list`.
+  - [x] **Citations**: Import and add `Citation` records referenced in `citation_ref_list`.
+  - [x] **Sources**: When importing citations, resolve and import the parent `Source` records.
+  - [x] **Repositories**: When importing sources, resolve and import the linked `Repository` records.
+
+---
+
+## [MEDIUM PRIORITY] 2. Missing GEDCOM Hierarchy Elements
+- [ ] **Family-Level Events**:
+  - [ ] Add comparison rows for family events: Marriage (`MARR`), Divorce (`DIV`), etc.
+  - [ ] Copy and merge family-level events when linking spouses or joining families.
+- [ ] **Alternate Names**:
+  - [ ] Compare alternate names between source and target persons.
+  - [ ] Add a resolution/arrow to copy alternate names to the target person's name list.
+- [ ] **Personal Attributes**:
+  - [ ] Compare personal attributes (e.g. Occupation, Residence, Education, Religion) stored in `attribute_list`.
+  - [ ] Add resolutions to copy these attributes during merges.
+- [ ] **Place Deduplication**:
+  - [ ] Enhance `get_or_create_place` to compare coordinates (latitude/longitude) and hierarchy instead of just matching by title.
+
+---
+
+## [LOW PRIORITY] 3. UI/UX Enhancements & Optimization
+- [ ] **Optimized Linear Matching**: Maintain and scale the soundex + initial indexing structure for rapid match lookups on extremely large GEDCOM databases.
+- [ ] **Deduplication Warnings**: Provide user alerts if a relative being added (father/mother/spouse/child) already has a relationship slot filled in the target tree.
+- [ ] **Help & Tooltips**: Add contextual help documentation specifically explaining how the selective merge resolutions affect the target database.

+#

+# MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the
+# GNU General Public License for more details.
+#
+# You should have received a copy of the GNU General Public License along
+# with this program; if not, write to the Free Software Foundation, Inc.,
+# 51 Franklin Street, Fifth Floor, Boston, MA 02110-1301 USA.
+#
+
+"""Build the standalone ``GWizardFileMerge.zip`` addon package.
+
+The GWizard plugin version is controlled by the ``--version`` command-line
+argument (default: 0.0.1).
+
+Collects the GWizard backend, GUI, and tool modules scattered across
+``gramps/gen/gwizard``, ``gramps/gui/gwizard``, and ``gramps/plugins/tool``
+into a single flat ``GWizardFileMerge/`` addon directory inside a zip file,
+rewriting the ``gramps.gen.gwizard`` / ``gramps.gui.gwizard`` absolute
+imports and intra-package relative imports to flat sibling imports so the
+bundle loads under Gramps' plugin importer (which imports the tool module
+top-level via ``__import__`` with the addon directory on ``sys.path``).
+Also embeds the standalone addon registration file
+``GWizardFileMerge/GWizardFileMerge.gpr.py`` (mirroring the ``gwizardmerge``
+entry in ``gramps/plugins/tool/tools.gpr.py``). The unit tests are copied
+into ``GWizardFileMerge/tests/`` inside the bundle, with the same GWizard
+import rewriting applied so they resolve the flat sibling modules, and the
+test fixture tree (the ``.ged`` source fixture and its ``media/`` images)
+is copied verbatim into ``GWizardFileMerge/tests/data/`` so the
+fixture-based tests run against the extracted bundle instead of skipping.
+Newlines are normalized to LF so the output is identical regardless of the
+checkout's line-ending setting.
+"""
+
+# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+#
+# Standard Python modules
+#
+# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+from __future__ import annotations
+import argparse
+import logging
+import re
+import subprocess
+import zipfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+LOG = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+PACKAGE = "GWizardFileMerge"
+
+GPR_FILENAME = "GWizardFileMerge.gpr.py"
+
+# Standalone addon registration file, embedded in the bundle as
+# ``GWizardFileMerge/GWizardFileMerge.gpr.py``. This template is the single
+# source of truth for the generated registration; no standalone GPR file is
+# written to the repository.
+GPR_TEMPLATE = """\
+# Gramps registration file for the GWizard File Merge tool.
+
+from gramps.gen.plug._pluginreg import (
+    TOOL,
+    TOOL_DBPROC,
+    TOOL_MODE_GUI,
+    EXPERIMENTAL,
+    EXPERT,
+)
+from gramps.gen.const import GRAMPS_LOCALE as glocale
+from gramps.version import major_version, VERSION_TUPLE
+
+_ = glocale.translation.gettext
+
+if (5, 2, 0) <= VERSION_TUPLE <= (6, 2, 0):
+    register(
+        TOOL,
+        id="gwizardmerge",
+        name=_("GWizard File Merge"),
+        description=_(
+            "Family Tree Processing Tool to compare another genealogy file "
+            "(GEDCOM, Gramps XML, ...) side-by-side with the open Family Tree "
+            "and merge selected differences person by person."
+        ),
+        version="__GWIZARD_VERSION__",
+        gramps_target_version=major_version,
+        status=EXPERIMENTAL,
+        audience=EXPERT,
+        fname="gwizardmerge.py",
+        authors=["Kevin White"],
+        authors_email=["gocaveman@gmail.com"],
+        category=TOOL_DBPROC,
+        toolclass="GWizardMergeTool",
+        optionclass="GWizardMergeToolOptions",
+        tool_modes=[TOOL_MODE_GUI],
+        help_url=("https://gramps-project.org/wiki/index.php/Addon:GWizardFileMerge_Tool")
+    )
+"""
+
+# Contents of ``GWizardFileMerge/tests/__init__.py`` inside the bundle. The
+# bundled tests use flat sibling imports (``from gwizard import ...``), which
+# only resolve when the addon directory itself is on ``sys.path``. Gramps puts
+# it there when loading the plugin, but a plain
+# ``python3 -m unittest GWizardFileMerge.tests.<module>`` run from the
+# ``addons-source`` directory does not, so the package initializer adds it.
+TESTS_INIT = """\
+\"\"\"Make the flat GWizard addon modules importable when running the tests.\"\"\"
+import os
+import sys
+
+_ADDON_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+if _ADDON_DIR not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, _ADDON_DIR)
+"""
+
+DEFAULT_GRAMPS_TARGET = "6.0"
+
+# Map of archive member name -> source file relative to the repo root.
+SOURCES: dict[str, str] = {
+    "gwizardgedcom.py": "gramps/gen/gwizard/gwizardgedcom.py",
+    "gwizard_base.py": "gramps/gen/gwizard/gwizard_base.py",
+    "gwizard_base.py": "gramps/gen/gwizard/gwizard_base.py",
+    "gwizard_base.py": "gramps/gen/gwizard/gwizard_base.py",
+    "gwizardmerge.py": "gramps/plugins/tool/gwizardmerge.py",
+    "gwizardmergedialog.py": "gramps/gui/gwizard/gwizardmergedialog.py",
+}
+
+# Unit-test modules bundled into ``GWizardFileMerge/tests/`` inside the zip.
+# Keyed by test file name; the value is the source path relative to the repo
+# root. These are placed under the ``tests/`` subdirectory rather than flat,
+# and receive the same GWizard import rewriting as the tool modules.
+TEST_SOURCES: dict[str, str] = {
+    "gwizard_test.py": "gramps/gen/gwizard/test/gwizard_test.py",
+    "gwizard_merge_fields_test.py": (
+        "gramps/gui/gwizard/test/gwizard_merge_fields_test.py"
+    ),
+    "gwizard_styling_test.py": "gramps/gui/gwizard/test/gwizard_styling_test.py",
+    "gwizardmerge_test.py": "gramps/plugins/tool/test/gwizardmerge_test.py",
+}
+
+# Fixture tree bundled into ``GWizardFileMerge/tests/data/`` inside the zip,
+# relative to the repo root. The bundled ``GWizardSourceMediaMergeTest``
+# resolves it as ``os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "data")`` next to
+# ``gwizard_test.py``, and the GEDCOM's relative ``FILE media/...`` paths
+# resolve against the ``.ged`` file's own directory, so the whole tree is
+# copied across preserving its layout. Files are copied verbatim (no import
+# rewriting): text files only get newline normalization, and the ``.png``
+# images are byte-for-byte copies since import rewriting assumes UTF-8 text.
+TEST_DATA_DIR = "gramps/gen/gwizard/test/data"
+
+# GWizard sibling modules bundled flat in the addon directory. Gramps loads
+# the tool module top-level (``__import__("gwizardmerge")`` with the addon
+# directory on ``sys.path``), so intra-bundle imports must be flat as well:
+# both ``gramps.gen.gwizard.X`` / ``gramps.gui.gwizard.X`` absolute imports
+# and ``.X`` relative imports become ``X`` sibling imports.
+SIBLINGS = (
+    "gwizardgedcom",
+    "gwizard",
+    "gwizardcompare",
+    "gwizardlauncher",
+    "gwizardmerge",
+    "gwizardmergedialog",
+)
+
+# Absolute imports that must become flat sibling imports in the bundle.
+# Order matters: longest prefixes first.
+REWRITES: tuple[tuple[re.Pattern[str], str], ...] = (
+    (
+        re.compile(r"from\s+gramps\.gen\.gwizard\.gwizardgedcom\s+import\b"),
+        "from gwizardgedcom import",
+    ),
+    (
+        re.compile(r"from\s+gramps\.gen\.gwizard\.gwizard\s+import\b"),
+        "from gwizard import",
+    ),
+    (
+        re.compile(r"from\s+gramps\.gui\.gwizard\.gwizardcompare\s+import\b"),
+        "from gwizardcompare import",
+    ),
+    (
+        re.compile(
+            r"from\s+gramps\.gui\.gwizard\.gwizardlauncher\s+import\b" r"(?!\s*\()",
+        ),
+        "from gwizardlauncher import",
+    ),
+    (
+        re.compile(r"from\s+gramps\.gui\.gwizard\.gwizardmergedialog\s+import\b"),
+        "from gwizardmergedialog import",
+    ),
+    # Relative imports: one or more leading dots both flatten to a flat
+    # sibling import -- single dot (``from .gwizard import``) as used by the
+    # tool modules, double dot (``from ..gwizard import``) as used by the
+    # bundled unit tests.
+    (
+        re.compile(
+            r"from\s+\.+(gwizardgedcom|gwizard|gwizardcompare|gwizardlauncher|"
+            r"gwizardmerge|gwizardmergedialog)\s+import\b"
+        ),
+        r"from \1 import",
+    ),
+    # Module-from-package imports (``from gramps.gui.gwizard import
+    # gwizardmergedialog``) become a bare ``import <module>`` sibling import.
+    (
+        re.compile(
+            r"from\s+gramps\.(?:gen|gui)\.gwizard\s+import\s+"
+            r"(gwizardgedcom|gwizard|gwizardcompare|gwizardlauncher|"
+            r"gwizardmerge|gwizardmergedialog)\b"
+        ),
+        r"import \1",
+    ),
+)
+
+
+def rewrite_imports(text: str) -> str:
+    """Rewrite GWizard imports to flat sibling imports."""
+    for pattern, replacement in REWRITES:
+        text = pattern.sub(replacement, text)
+    return text
+
+
+def rewrite_imports_bytes(data: bytes) -> bytes:
+    """Rewrite GWizard imports, normalizing to LF newlines."""
+    lines = data.decode("utf-8").splitlines(keepends=False)
+    rewritten = [rewrite_imports(line) for line in lines]
+    return ("\n".join(rewritten) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
+
+
+def build_gpr(version: str = "0.0.1") -> bytes:
+    """Render the standalone addon registration file."""
+    return GPR_TEMPLATE.replace("__GWIZARD_VERSION__", version).encode("utf-8")
+
+
+def build_test_data(repo_root: Path) -> dict[str, bytes]:
+    """
+    Collect the test fixture tree for ``tests/data/`` in the bundle.
+
+    :param repo_root: Repository root holding the fixture tree.
+    :returns: Map of archive-relative POSIX path to file bytes.
+    :raises FileNotFoundError: If the fixture tree or GEDCOM fixture is
+        missing from the repository.
+    """
+    data_root = repo_root / TEST_DATA_DIR
+    if not data_root.is_dir():
+        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing test data directory: {data_root}")
+    ged_fixture = data_root / "gwizard_mergefrom_sources.ged"
+    if not ged_fixture.is_file():
+        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing test fixture file: {ged_fixture}")
+    members: dict[str, bytes] = {}
+    for path in sorted(data_root.rglob("*")):
+        if not path.is_file():
+            continue
+        member = path.relative_to(data_root).as_posix()
+        raw = path.read_bytes()
+        if path.suffix.lower() == ".png":
+            # Binary image: byte-for-byte copy, import rewriting would
+            # corrupt it (rewrite_imports_bytes decodes UTF-8 text).
+            members[member] = raw
+        else:
+            # Text fixture (GEDCOM, generator script): normalize newlines
+            # like the code members so the bundle is deterministic.
+            lines = raw.decode("utf-8").splitlines(keepends=False)
+            members[member] = ("\n".join(lines) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
+        LOG.debug("Added data %s (%d bytes)", member, len(members[member]))
+    return members
+
+
+def build_translations(repo_root: Path) -> bytes:
+    """
+    Generate the translation template (po/template.pot) from source .py files.
+
+    This function extracts translatable strings (wrapped in ``_()``) from the
+    source .py files that are bundled in the zip (not the test files) and
+    generates a valid ``.pot`` (portable object template) file.
+
+    The standalone addon registration file (GWizardFileMerge.gpr.py) is
+    generated separately by ``build_gpr()``. This function instead produces
+    the gettext translation template so the addon can be translated by
+    translators.
+
+    :param repo_root: Repository root holding the source files.
+    :returns: The rendered translation template content.
+    """
+    import ast
+    import datetime
+
+    # Files that go into the bundle (not the test files).
+    source_files = [
+        "gramps/gen/gwizard/gwizardgedcom.py",
+        "gramps/gen/gwizard/gwizard_base.py",
+        "gramps/gui/gwizard/gwizardcompare.py",
+        "gramps/gui/gwizard/gwizardlauncher.py",
+        "gramps/plugins/tool/gwizardmerge.py",
+        "gramps/gui/gwizard/gwizardmergedialog.py",
+    ]
+
+    # Collect translatable strings from each source file.
+    # Each entry is (file_path, line_number, string_value).
+    entries: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
+    seen: set[str] = set()
+    for rel_path in source_files:
+        full_path = repo_root / rel_path
+        if not full_path.is_file():
+            continue
+        source_code = full_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        try:
+            tree = ast.parse(source_code, filename=rel_path)
+        except SyntaxError:
+            continue
+        for node in ast.walk(tree):
+            if not isinstance(node, ast.Call):
+                continue
+            func = node.func
+            # Match calls to _(...) for translation.
+            if isinstance(func, ast.Name) and func.id == "_":
+                if node.args:
+                    arg = node.args[0]
+                    msgid: str | None = None
+                    if isinstance(arg, ast.Constant) and isinstance(arg.value, str):
+                        msgid = arg.value
+                    elif isinstance(arg, ast.BinOp) and isinstance(arg.op, ast.Mod):
+                        # Handle "string % var" pattern.
+                        if isinstance(arg.left, ast.Constant) and isinstance(
+                            arg.left.value, str
+                        ):
+                            msgid = arg.left.value
+                    if msgid is not None and msgid not in seen:
+                        seen.add(msgid)
+                        entries.append((rel_path, node.lineno, msgid))
+
+    # Generate the .pot file content.
+    now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M%z")
+    lines: list[str] = [
+        "# SOME DESCRIPTIVE TITLE.",
+        "# Copyright (C) YEAR THE PACKAGE'S COPYRIGHT HOLDER",
+        "# This file is distributed under the same license as the PACKAGE package.",
+        "# FIRST AUTHOR <EMAIL@ADDRESS>, YEAR.",
+        "#",
+        "#, fuzzy",
+        'msgid ""',
+        'msgstr ""',
+        f'"Project-Id-Version: PACKAGE VERSION\\n"',
+        '"Report-Msgid-Bugs-To: \\n"',
+        f'"POT-Creation-Date: {now}\\n"',
+        '"PO-Revision-Date: YEAR-MO-DA HO:MI+ZONE\\n"',
+        '"Last-Translator: FULL NAME <EMAIL@ADDRESS>\\n"',
+        '"Language-Team: LANGUAGE <LL@li.org>\\n"',
+        '"Language: \\n"',
+        '"MIME-Version: 1.0\\n"',
+        '"Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8\\n"',
+        '"Content-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit\\n"',
+        '"Plural-Forms: nplurals=INTEGER; plural=EXPRESSION;\\n"',
+        "",
+    ]
+
+    for file_path, line_no, msgid in entries:
+        lines.append(f"#: {file_path}:{line_no}")
+        # Escape special characters in msgid.
+        escaped = msgid.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"')
+        lines.append(f'msgid "{escaped}"')
+        lines.append('msgstr ""')
+        lines.append("")
+
+    content = "\n".join(lines).encode("utf-8")
+
+    # Create the po/ directory and write po/template.pot.
+    po_dir = repo_root / "po"
+    po_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    target_po = po_dir / "template.pot"
+    target_po.write_bytes(content)
+
+    result = target_po.read_bytes()
+
+    target_po.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+    try:
+        po_dir.rmdir()
+    except OSError:
+        pass
+
+    return result
+
+
+def build_zip(repo_root: Path, output: Path, version: str = "0.0.1") -> Path:
+    """Build the ``GWizardFileMerge.zip`` bundle next to this script."""
+    members: dict[str, bytes] = {}
+    for member, source in SOURCES.items():
+        path = repo_root / source
+        if not path.is_file():
+            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing source file: {path}")
+        text = path.read_bytes()
+        members[member] = rewrite_imports_bytes(text)
+        LOG.debug("Added %s (%d bytes)", source, len(members[member]))
+
+    test_members: dict[str, bytes] = {}
+    for member, source in TEST_SOURCES.items():
+        path = repo_root / source
+        if not path.is_file():
+            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing test source file: {path}")
+        text = path.read_bytes()
+        test_members[member] = rewrite_imports_bytes(text)
+        LOG.debug("Added test %s (%d bytes)", source, len(test_members[member]))
+
+    data_members = build_test_data(repo_root)
+
+    output.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(output, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
+        archive.writestr(PACKAGE + "/", b"")
+        archive.writestr(f"{PACKAGE}/{GPR_FILENAME}", build_gpr(version))
+        for member in sorted(members):
+            archive.writestr(f"{PACKAGE}/{member}", members[member])
+        if test_members:
+            archive.writestr(PACKAGE + "/tests/", b"")
+            archive.writestr(PACKAGE + "/tests/__init__.py", TESTS_INIT.encode("utf-8"))
+            for member in sorted(test_members):
+                archive.writestr(f"{PACKAGE}/tests/{member}", test_members[member])
+        if data_members:
+            archive.writestr(f"{PACKAGE}/tests/data/", b"")
+            for member in sorted(data_members):
+                archive.writestr(f"{PACKAGE}/tests/data/{member}", data_members[member])
+
+        # Include the translation template (po/template.pot) generated from the
+        # source .py files.
+        po_template = build_translations(repo_root)
+        archive.writestr(f"{PACKAGE}/po/template.pot", po_template)
+
+    LOG.info("Wrote %s (%d bytes)", output, output.stat().st_size)
+    return output
+
+
+def parse_args(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> argparse.Namespace:
+    """Parse command-line arguments."""
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
+        description="Build the standalone GWizardFileMerge.zip addon package."
+    )
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "-o",
+        "--output",
+        type=Path,
+        default=None,
+        help="Destination zip file (default: GWizardFileMerge_v<version>.zip next to the script, where <version> is the plugin version).",
+    )
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "--gramps-target",
+        default=DEFAULT_GRAMPS_TARGET,
+        help=(
+            "Accepted for compatibility; the bundled registration file now "
+            "uses Gramps' dynamic major_version with a VERSION_TUPLE gate, "
+            f"so this value (default: {DEFAULT_GRAMPS_TARGET}) is ignored."
+        ),
+    )
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "--version",
+        dest="version_opt",
+        default="0.0.1",
+        help="GWizard plugin version for the registration file (default: 0.0.1).",
+    )
+    parser.add_argument(
+        "version_pos",
+        nargs="?",
+        default=None,
+        help="GWizard plugin version for the registration file (default: 0.0.1), "
+        "alternative to --version.",
+    )
+    return parser.parse_args(argv)
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
+    """Build the bundle and report the result."""
+    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(message)s")
+    args = parse_args(argv)
+    repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+    # Bare positional version takes precedence over --version; both default to
+    # "0.0.1" when neither is supplied.
+    version = args.version_pos if args.version_pos is not None else args.version_opt
+    # Default output name embeds the plugin version: GWizardFileMerge_v<version>.zip.
+    if args.output is None:
+        output = repo_root / f"GWizardFileMerge_v{version}.zip"
+    else:
+        output = args.output
+        if not output.is_absolute():
+            output = repo_root / output
+    try:
+        built = build_zip(repo_root, output, version=version)
+    except FileNotFoundError as error:
+        LOG.error("%s", error)
+        return 1
+    print(str(built))
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())
